Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the thermodynamics space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 856 and the convergence rate is O(530/n^3).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 856 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the thermodynamics structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 530. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
